=== tab_switch
tab 1 hint=Download saves a mod into your mods folder; Get saves a pack to the Modpacks tab.
Switch between Mods and Modpacks at the left; the source menu picks the mod site.
tab 2 hint=Apply switches you to the pack's mods and settings; Unload brings your previous setup back.
Find more packs under Browse > Modpacks.
tab 0 hint=Higher number loads later and wins when mods share files.
Required dependencies must be enabled or the mod won't load.
